# THE SHIPPED SHADER CORPUS, as the toolchain in this tree emits it.
# One row per artefact: <program>.<stage>.<target> <sha256> <size>, and one
# <program>.layout.<target> row per program carrying the reflected shape in full.
# Size is bytes for SPIR-V, characters for emitted text, elements for a layout.
#
# WHY IT EXISTS. Veldrid.SPIRV and Silk.NET.Shaderc cannot be loaded into one process
# (section 2.3 result 4 of docs/design/VELDRID-REMOVAL-DESIGN-2026-08-22.md), so no
# test can compare the outgoing toolchain with the incoming one. This file and its
# corpus.veldrid-spirv.txt sibling are that comparison, taken in two processes.
#
# Rebuild: KE_WRITE_SHADER_CORPUS=1 dotnet test KhaozEngine.Render.Tests --filter ShaderCorpus
# Rows: 1160
